Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 288, 'covered': 288, 'checked': 8, 'passed': 8}
    TITLE = 'Provenance'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-AVCPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

  • JAH Network AI Access guide (docs/AI_ACCESS.md) — The documented read paths (registry, mirrors, record API, MCP) each capability is measured against.
  • JAH Network Master List (ChatGPT catalog, 2026-11-12) — The catalog requirement each system capability is specified from; catalog text is held package-only in the master-list program directory.

Licensing JAH-AVCPY-000045 · complex

Evidence: established Public record: the Python generator described here was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2); its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte. Facts about the companion database are verified against the live database it companions.

Licensing states the terms plainly: the companion generators are Signature-made programs, free to read and run, and the public edition's generated records carry public-harvest provenance.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database animal-vocal-communication.

Description

Licensing states the terms plainly: the companion generators are Signature-made programs, free to read and run, and the public edition's generated records carry public-harvest provenance.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Animal Vocal Communication Python Generator licensing — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

State the license in the pattern and in each record's evidence label (established vs design-study). Generated records are labeled by edition, never mixed.

The Answer

Licensing is by the network's standing rules (public = real fact-checked information; Signature = original versions, labeled design-study).

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 288, 'covered': 288, 'checked': 8, 'passed': 8}
TITLE = 'Licensing'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-AVCPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Boundless scales JAH-AVCPY-000046 · simple

Evidence: established Public record: the Python generator described here was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2); its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte. Facts about the companion database are verified against the live database it companions.

Boundless scales are the generator's promise: the same program generates at A1 (one database), A2 (the network), and A3 (every scale of every file) — generated on demand, never stored.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database animal-vocal-communication.

Description

Boundless scales are the generator's promise: the same program generates at A1 (one database), A2 (the network), and A3 (every scale of every file) — generated on demand, never stored.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Animal Vocal Communication Python Generator boundless scales — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Keep the generator seed-based so any scale is reachable by seed range; the ceiling is 1,000,000,000 scales per file. Scales are generated, not stored, and the record's scales field states all three.

The Answer

Scales A1/A2/A3 are stated in all 288 pilot records; no scale beyond the pilot topics was generated or stored.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

FIGS = {'total': 1000000000, 'covered': 288, 'checked': 8, 'passed': 8}
TITLE = 'Boundless scales'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-AVCPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 1000000000, 'covered': 288, 'checked': 8, 'passed': 8}
    TITLE = 'Boundless scales'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-AVCPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Registry entry JAH-AVCPY-000047 · compounded

Evidence: established Public record: the Python generator described here was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2); its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte. Facts about the companion database are verified against the live database it companions.

The registry entry lists the companion generator where it can be found — its database, its prefix, its contract version — so any AI can discover it without scanning 63,048 files.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database animal-vocal-communication.

Description

The registry entry lists the companion generator where it can be found — its database, its prefix, its contract version — so any AI can discover it without scanning 63,048 files.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Animal Vocal Communication Python Generator registry entry — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Publish one entry per companion generator in the companion manifest (manifest-entries.json), with its companion target and its language. Entries are built from the real build, never hand-written.

The Answer

Registry entries exist as the pilots' manifest-entries.json (6 entries); no network-wide generator registry entry exists yet for companions.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 3, 'covered': 3, 'checked': 8, 'passed': 8}
TITLE = 'Registry entry'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-AVCPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)

        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),

Companion contract JAH-AVCPY-000048 · complex

Evidence: established Public record: the Python generator described here was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2); its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte. The companion contract is this pattern itself (v1.0.0): the eight rules in PATTERN.md §3 that every companion generator must satisfy, with the template as the reference implementation.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database animal-vocal-communication.

Description

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Animal Vocal Communication Python Generator companion contract — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Check every new companion against the eight rules at build time; a companion that fails any rule is not accepted. The contract is versioned, and a new version re-checks every existing companion.

The Answer

The contract is proven by the three pilots (288 records, 288 demos re-executed, 0 problems) and the template (8/8 self-tests).

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

The contract is proven by the three pilots (288 records, 288 demos re-executed, 0 problems) and the template (8/8 self-tests).

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 8, 'covered': 3, 'passed': 8, 'total': 3}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Working demo program

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 3, 'covered': 3, 'checked': 8, 'passed': 8}
TITLE = 'Companion contract'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-AVCPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 3, 'covered': 3, 'checked': 8, 'passed': 8}
    TITLE = 'Companion contract'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-AVCPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())
